### [67] [Biomedical systems biology workflow orchestration and execution with PoSyMed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20906)
*Simon Süwer,Zoe Chervontseva,Kester Bagemihl,Jan Baumbach,Olga Tsoy,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PoSyMed是一个开源模块化平台，通过结合后端架构、容器化执行、形式化工具描述、持久化工作流状态和基于LLM的有限语义助手，解决了生物信息学软件在集成、复现和工作流适配方面的障碍，旨在提升生物医学分析的可复现性、可追溯性和透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物信息学研究面临科学软件快速增长带来的实际障碍：碎片化分发、不一致的文档、复杂的依赖关系和难以复现的执行环境，导致即使有经验的用户也难以复现已发布工具和适配工作流。

Method: PoSyMed采用后端中心化架构，结合形式化工具描述、基于容器的受控构建与执行流程、持久化工作流状态以及对话式用户界面。LLM被集成作为有限语义助手，帮助识别工具、建议工作流步骤和支持参数化，但并非自主决策者，所有操作均在类型化、经过验证且受人类监督的执行环境中进行。

Result: 在代表性生物学软件场景中评估了平台的工作流支持、交互设计和可扩展性，平台已公开发布。

Conclusion: PoSyMed通过单一平台改善了实际生物医学分析中的可复现性、可追溯性和透明度，将LLM作为受控的人机接口而非自主决策者，在保证人类监督的前提下提升了工具和工作流的使用效率。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific software has created practical barriers for bioinformatics research. Although powerful statistical, artificial intelligence (AI)-based methods are now widely available, their effective use is often hindered by fragmented distribution, inconsistent documentation, complex dependencies, and difficult-to-reproduce execution environments. As a result, reusing published tools and workflow adaptation to own date remains technically demanding and time-intensive, even for experienced users. Here, we present PoSyMed, an open and modular platform for the controlled integration, composition, and execution of bioinformatics tools and workflows. PoSyMed combines a backend-centered platform architecture with formal tool descriptions, controlled container-based build and execution processes, persistent workflow state, and a dialogue-based user interface. Large language models (LLM) are integrated not as autonomous decision-makers, but as human-computer interface with bounded semantic assistants that help identify tools, propose workflow steps, and support parameterization within a typed, validated, and human-supervised execution environment. PoSyMed is designed to improve reproducibility, traceability, and transparency in practical biomedical analysis within one platform. We describe the system architecture and evaluate its behavior across representative biological software scenarios with respect to workflow support, interaction design, and platform extensibility. PoSyMed is publicly available at https://apps.cosy.bio/posymed.

</details>


### [68] [Residual Risk Analysis in Benign Code: How Far Are We? A Multi-Model Semantic and Structural Similarity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21051)
*Mohammad Farhad,Shuvalaxmi Dass*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Residual Risk Scoring (RRS)框架，通过语义和结构相似性分析，发现PrimeVul数据集中约61%的高风险代码对存在13类残留安全问题（如空指针解引用、不安全内存分配），表明修补后的函数仍可能残留安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞基准通常将修补后的函数视为完全安全的，忽略了残留安全风险的可能性，缺乏系统性评估补丁是否彻底消除风险的方法。

Method: 使用多个代码语言模型（Code LMs）计算语义相似度，结合Tree-sitter的抽象语法树（AST）分析计算结构相似度，构建统一框架RRS，整合嵌入级语义相似性、局部AST结构相似性和跨模型一致性来估计代码中的残留风险。

Result: 良性函数与脆弱函数在语义和结构上仍高度相似；约61%的高RRS代码对存在13类残留问题，经Cppcheck、Clang-Tidy、Facebook-Infer等静态分析工具验证。

Conclusion: 代码级相似性可作为补丁后检查的实用信号，实现更可靠、可扩展的软件安全评估。

Abstract: Software security relies on effective vulnerability detection and patching, yet determining whether a patch fully eliminates risk remains an underexplored challenge. Existing vulnerability benchmarks often treat patched functions as inherently benign, overlooking the possibility of residual security risks. In this work, we analyze vulnerable-benign function pairs from the PrimeVul, a benchmark dataset using multiple code language models (Code LMs) to capture semantic similarity, complemented by Tree-sitter-based abstract syntax tree (AST) analysis for structural similarity. Building on these signals, we propose Residual Risk Scoring (RRS), a unified framework that integrates embedding-based semantic similarity, localized AST-based structural similarity, and cross-model agreement to estimate residual risk in code. Our analysis shows that benign functions often remain highly similar to their vulnerable counterparts both semantically and structurally, indicating potential persistence of residual risk. We further find that approximately $61\%$ of high-RRS code pairs exhibit $13$ distinct categories of residual issues (e.g., null pointer dereferences, unsafe memory allocation), validated using state-of-the-art static analysis tools including Cppcheck, Clang-Tidy, and Facebook-Infer. These results demonstrate that code-level similarity provides a practical signal for prioritizing post-patch inspection, enabling more reliable and scalable security assessment in real-world open-source software pipelines.

</details>


### [69] [Structural Quality Gaps in Practitioner AI Governance Prompts: An Empirical Study Using a Five-Principle Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21090)
*Christo Zietsman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于可计算性理论、证明论和贝叶斯认识论的五原则评估框架，用于评估AI治理提示(prompts)的结构完整性。通过对34份来自GitHub的AGENTS.md治理文件进行实证分析，发现37%的文件-模型对低于结构完整性阈值，其中数据分类和评估标准最常缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自然语言提示(prompts)作为可执行规范在AI治理中扮演关键角色，定义了智能体的授权范围和质量标准，但缺乏系统的框架来评估治理提示的结构完整性。

Method: 基于可计算性理论、证明论和贝叶斯认识论构建五原则评估框架；对34份公开的AGENTS.md治理文件进行实证语料分析。

Result: 37%的文件-模型对未达到结构完整性阈值；数据分类和评估标准是最常缺失的要素。

Conclusion: 实践者编写的治理提示存在一致的结构模式，可通过自动化静态分析检测和修复；讨论了对AI辅助开发中需求工程实践的启示，识别了AGENTS.md约定中此前未记录的工件分类空白，并提出了工具支持方向。

Abstract: AI governance programmes increasingly rely on natural language prompts to constrain and direct AI agent behaviour. These prompts function as executable specifications: they define the agent's mandate, scope, and quality criteria. Despite this role, no systematic framework exists for evaluating whether a governance prompt is structurally complete. We introduce a five-principle evaluation framework grounded in computability theory, proof theory, and Bayesian epistemology, and apply it to an empirical corpus of 34 publicly available AGENTS.md governance files sourced from GitHub. Our evaluation reveals that 37% of evaluated file-model pairs score below the structural completeness threshold, with data classification and assessment rubric criteria most frequently absent. These results suggest that practitioner-authored governance prompts exhibit consistent structural patterns that automated static analysis could detect and remediate. We discuss implications for requirements engineering practice in AI-assisted development contexts, identify a previously undocumented artefact classification gap in the AGENTS.md convention, and propose directions for tool support.

</details>


### [70] [A Ground-Truth-Based Evaluation of Vulnerability Detection Across Multiple Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21111)
*Peter Mandl,Paul Mandl,Martin Häusl,Maximilian Auch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文利用OSV数据库构建了跨软件生态系统的漏洞检测基准数据集，对不同工具的检测结果进行了实证评估，发现系统间存在显著差异，并提供了可复现数据集的开源工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化漏洞检测工具的评估面临数据源结构异构、标识符方案不一致以及版本范围规范模糊等挑战，缺乏统一的、可复现的评估基准。

Method: 基于Open Source Vulnerabilities (OSV)数据库构建了将漏洞映射到具体软件包版本的地面真值数据集，采用快照方式捕获评估时的漏洞状态，并开发了开源工具以支持数据集的自动重建与结果对比。

Result: 不同漏洞检测系统之间存在系统性差异；透明化的数据集构建方法对可复现的实证安全研究至关重要。

Conclusion: 漏洞检测工具的评估高度依赖底层数据质量，标准化的数据集构建和版本映射机制是提升实证研究可复现性和可比性的关键。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability detection tools are widely used to identify security vulnerabilities in software dependencies. However, the evaluation of such tools remains challenging due to the heterogeneous structure of vulnerability data sources, inconsistent identifier schemes, and ambiguities in version range specifications. In this paper, we present an empirical evaluation of vulnerability detection across multiple software ecosystems using a curated ground-truth dataset derived from the Open Source Vulnerabilities (OSV) database. The dataset explicitly maps vulnerabilities to concrete package versions and enables a systematic comparison of detection results across different tools and services. Since vulnerability databases such as OSV are continuously updated, the dataset used in this study represents a snapshot of the vulnerability landscape at the time of the evaluation. To support reproducibility and future studies, we provide an open-source tool that automatically reconstructs the dataset from the current OSV database using the methodology described in this paper. Our evaluation highlights systematic differences between vulnerability detection systems and demonstrates the importance of transparent dataset construction for reproducible empirical security research.

</details>


### [71] [Hidden Dependencies and Component Variants in SBOM-Based Software Composition Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21278)
*Shawn Rasheed,Max McPhee,Lisa Patterson,Stephen MacDonell,Jens Dietrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了SBOM中两类不匹配模式：隐藏的代码级依赖关系和组件变体（克隆），这些不匹配导致不同SBOM漏洞扫描器在漏洞报告和VEX声明处理上出现不一致，揭示了当前SBOM生成和消费的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBOM作为供应链安全的关键技术，其有效性依赖于对组件身份和实际依赖关系的准确表示，但现有SBOM存在不匹配问题，影响漏洞管理的可靠性。

Method: 识别并分析两类不匹配模式：1）隐藏的代码级依赖关系未表示为组件级依赖；2）组件变体（克隆）无法被扫描器一致识别。通过对比流行SBOM漏洞扫描器在不匹配场景下的表现进行实证研究。

Result: 这些不匹配导致不同SBOM漏洞扫描器在漏洞报告和VEX声明处理上出现不一致的结果。

Conclusion: 当前SBOM的依赖表示和组件标识机制存在局限，需要更丰富的依赖表示和组件身份识别方法来提升SBOM在漏洞管理中的有效性。

Abstract: Software Bills of Material (SBOMs) have emerged as an important technology for vulnerability management amid rising supply-chain attacks. They represent component relationships within a software product and support software composition analysis (SCA) by linking components to known vulnerabilities. However, the effectiveness of SBOM-based analysis depends on how accurately SBOMs represent component identities and actual dependencies in software. This paper studies two mismatch patterns: hidden code-level dependencies that are not represented as component-level dependencies, and component variants (clones) that cannot be identified consistently by scanners. We show that these mismatches can lead to inconsistent vulnerability reporting and inconsistent handling of VEX statements across popular SBOM-based vulnerability scanners. These results highlight limitations in current SBOM production and consumption and motivate richer dependency representation and component identity.

</details>


### [72] [Conjecture and Inquiry: Quantifying Software Performance Requirements via Interactive Retrieval-Augmented Preference Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21380)
*Wang Shi Hai,Chen Tao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: IRAP通过交互式检索增强偏好 elicitation，将自然语言描述的性能需求自动量化为数学函数，在四个真实数据集上相比10种最先进方法取得最高40倍提升，且仅需不超过5轮交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件性能需求以自然语言记录，需量化为数学形式才能用于软件工程。然而，性能需求中的模糊性和人类认知的不确定性导致解释存在高度不确定的歧义，使得自动化量化成为一个未解决且极具挑战的问题。

Method: 形式化定义了性能需求量化问题，提出IRAP方法：通过交互式检索增强偏好 elicitation，显式利用问题特定知识检索和推理偏好，同时引导与利益相关者的渐进式交互，降低认知负担。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上对比10种最先进方法，IRAP在所有案例中均表现优越，在仅需不超过5轮交互的情况下实现高达40倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: IRAP有效解决了性能需求量化中的模糊性和认知不确定性挑战，通过知识驱动的交互式偏好推理实现了高效、精准的自动化量化。

Abstract: Since software performance requirements are documented in natural language, quantifying them into mathematical forms is essential for software engineering. Yet, the vagueness in performance requirements and uncertainty of human cognition have caused highly uncertain ambiguity in the interpretations, rendering their automated quantification an unaddressed and challenging problem. In this paper, we formalize the problem and propose IRAP, an approach that quantifies performance requirements into mathematical functions via interactive retrieval-augmented preference elicitation. IRAP differs from the others in that it explicitly derives from problem-specific knowledge to retrieve and reason the preferences, which also guides the progressive interaction with stakeholders, while reducing the cognitive overhead. Experiment results against 10 state-of-the-art methods on four real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of IRAP on all cases with up to 40x improvements under as few as five rounds of interactions.

</details>


### [73] [Assessing the Impact of Requirement Ambiguity on LLM-based Function-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21505)
*Di Yang,Xinou Xie,Xiuwen Yang,Ming Hu,Yihao Huang,Yueling Zhang,Weikai Miao,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan,Geguang Pu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向模糊需求代码生成的基准测试Orchid，包含1,304个覆盖四种歧义类型的函数级任务。实验发现：歧义会持续降低LLM的代码生成性能，且对先进模型影响更显著；LLM常为同一模糊需求生成功能分歧的实现，且缺乏自主识别或解决歧义的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试假设需求清晰无歧义，忽略了现实软件开发中自然语言需求固有的模糊性，导致对LLM在模糊需求下代码生成行为的研究存在实证空白。

Method: 构建Orchid基准：包含1,304个函数级任务，覆盖词汇歧义、句法歧义、语义歧义和模糊性四种类型。基于该数据集，对多种LLM进行系统性实证研究，评估需求模糊性对代码生成的影响。

Result: 模糊性持续降低所有评估LLM的性能，且对高级模型负面影响更显著；LLM常为同一模糊需求生成功能不同的实现，且无法自主识别或解决歧义。

Conclusion: 清晰需求与模糊需求之间存在显著的性能差距，亟需在下一代自动化软件工程工具中引入歧义感知技术。

Abstract: Software requirement ambiguity is ubiquitous in real-world development, stemming from the inherent imprecision of natural language and the varying interpretations of stakeholders. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generating code from precise specifications, such ambiguity poses a significant obstacle to reliable automated code generation. Existing benchmarks typically assume clear and unambiguous requirements, leaving an empirical gap in understanding how LLMs behave when faced with the inherent uncertainty of real-world software requirements. In this paper, we introduce Orchid, the first code generation benchmark specifically designed with ambiguous requirements. It comprises 1,304 function-level tasks covering four distinct types of ambiguity: lexical, syntactic, semantic, and vagueness. Leveraging this dataset, we conduct the first systematic empirical study to evaluate the impact of requirement ambiguity on LLM-based code generation. Our results demonstrate that ambiguity consistently degrades the performance of all evaluated LLMs, with the most pronounced negative effects observed in highly advanced models. Furthermore, we observe that LLMs frequently produce functionally divergent implementations for the same ambiguous requirement and lack the capability to identify or resolve such ambiguity autonomously. These findings reveal a significant performance gap between clear and ambiguous requirements, underscoring the urgent need for ambiguity-aware techniques in the next generation of automated software engineering tools. The Orchid benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/SII-YDD/Orchid.

</details>


### [74] [SpecSyn: LLM-based Synthesis and Refinement of Formal Specifications for Real-world Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21570)
*Lezhi Ma,Shangqing Liu,Yi Li,Qiong Wu,Han Wang,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpecSyn是一种基于大语言模型的程序规范自动生成方法，通过程序分解和基于语义变异区分的规范精化机制，实现了高精度（>90%）和高召回率（>75%），成功处理了1071/1365个真实程序的验证属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的规范生成方法缺乏处理大规模输入程序的有效策略，且没有评估和保证生成规范语义强度的机制，难以从真实复杂程序中提取精确规范以支持目标属性验证。

Method: SpecSyn首先将输入程序分解为独立片段，分别进行迭代式规范生成；创新性地引入基于语义非等价程序变异和变异区分的规范精化机制，评估并增强生成规范的语义强度。

Result: 精度超过90%，召回率超过75%，显著优于现有LLM方法；在真实开源程序验证中成功处理1365个目标属性中的1071个。

Conclusion: SpecSyn通过分解策略和语义变异精化机制有效提升了LLM生成程序规范的精度和召回率，证明了其在真实程序验证任务中的实用性。

Abstract: Program verification is a formal technique to rigorously ensure the correctness and fault-freeness of software systems. However, constructing comprehensive interprocedural specifications for full verification obligations is time-consuming and labor-intensive, giving rise to automated specification generation approaches. Despite the significant advancements in these approaches brought by Large Language Models (LLMs), existing LLM-empowered approaches still suffer from significant limitations: they lack effective strategies for handling sizable input programs, and are typically equipped with no mechanisms to evaluate and guarantee the strength of the generated specifications. The limitations impair their ability to extract precise specifications from real-world complicated programs to support the verification of target properties, thereby hindering the applicability of existing approaches in verification tasks on real-world programs. To remedy this gap, we propose SpecSyn, a novel LLM-based specification generation method. SpecSyn first decomposes the input program into individual segments, which are handled respectively by the subsequent iterative specification generation process. Innovatively, we incorporate into the process a specification refinement mechanism based on semantic-non-equivalent program mutations and variant discrimination, assessing and enhancing the semantic strength of the generated specifications. Extensive experiments show that SpecSyn maintains high precision over 90% and outstanding recall over 75%, significantly outperforming existing LLM-based approaches. In further evaluations, SpecSyn successfully handles 1071 out of 1365 target properties for open-source programs, proving its applicability on real-world program verification tasks.

</details>


### [75] [DryRUN: On the Role of Public Tests in LLM-Driven Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21598)
*Kaushitha Silva,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DryRUN框架，无需人工提供的公共测试用例即可通过LLM自主生成输入并模拟执行来调试代码，消除了对ground-truth样本的依赖，在LiveCodeBench v6数据集上达到了与依赖公共测试用例的SOTA方法CodeSIM相当的性能，同时减少了输出token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模拟驱动规划与调试的多智能体代码生成框架依赖人工编写的公共测试用例来指导调试循环，手工构造输入输出示例是软件开发生命周期中的劳动力密集瓶颈；且依赖公共测试会导致"过度自信差距"，使框架过拟合简单案例而在隐藏评估中失败。

Method: 提出DryRUN框架，核心思想是让LLM自主生成有效输入并模拟执行轨迹以进行自我修正，完全消除对ground-truth样本的需求。框架通过迭代规划、自主生成输入和模拟执行来缓解算法过度自信。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6数据集（2025年3月后数据）上，DryRUN达到了与CodeSIM（依赖公共测试用例的SOTA框架）相当的性能，且完全无需公共测试用例或外部执行反馈，同时减少了输出token消耗。

Conclusion: 外部样本输入对于代码生成并非严格必要，LLM可以自主生成有效输入并自我纠错；DryRUN验证了消除对人工测试用例依赖的可行性，为实际软件工程中的自动化代码生成提供了更实用的方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent frameworks are widely used in autonomous code generation and have applications in complex algorithmic problem-solving. Recent work has addressed the challenge of generating functionally correct code by incorporating simulation-driven planning and debugging, where language models trace execution steps to verify logic. However, these approaches depend on human-provided public test cases to ground the debugging and simulation loop. Manually authoring comprehensive input-output examples is a labor-intensive bottleneck in the software development lifecycle. Because ground-truth input-output examples are rarely available prior to implementation in real-world software engineering, this dependency restricts methods to curated competitive programming benchmarks. Furthermore, we identify that reliance on these public tests induces an ``overconfidence gap,'' causing frameworks to overfit to simplistic examples and fail on hidden evaluations. In contrast, we observe that external sample inputs are not strictly necessary for code generation. We demonstrate that large language models can autonomously generate valid inputs and simulate execution traces to self-correct. Consequently, we develop DryRUN, a framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth samples by allowing the LLM to iteratively plan, autonomously generate its own inputs and simulate execution, mitigating algorithmic overconfidence. Evaluations on the LiveCodeBench v6 dataset (post-March 2025) demonstrate that DryRUN matches performance against CodeSIM, a state-of-the-art and public-test-dependent framework, while operating entirely without public test cases or external execution feedback while reducing output token consumption.

</details>


### [76] [Verifying Machine Learning Interpretability Requirements through Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21599)
*Lynn Vonderhaar,Juan Couder,Daryela Cisneros,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用机器学习（ML）中的来源数据（provenance）来验证可解释性这一非功能需求（NFR），通过将不可度量的可解释性转化为可量化的功能需求（FRs）进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML工程中可解释性作为重要的非功能需求，但现有文献中可解释性是无法度量的需求，无法确认模型是否满足可解释性要求，亟需一种可验证的方法。

Method: 利用ML来源数据（provenance）——包括模型和数据的各种类型来源信息——使模型行为透明化与可解释；将这些来源数据作为可量化的功能需求的基础，通过验证功能需求来间接验证可解释性这一非功能需求。

Result: 提出了一种利用ML来源数据验证可解释性非功能需求的方法，将不可度量的可解释性需求转化为可验证的功能需求。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一种验证ML模型可解释性非功能需求的方法，通过保存模型和数据来源数据形成可量化的功能需求，从而实现对可解释性NFR的验证。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) Engineering is a growing field that necessitates an increase in the rigor of ML development. It draws many ideas from software engineering and more specifically, from requirements engineering. Existing literature on ML Engineering defines quality models and Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs) specific to ML, in particular interpretability being one such NFR. However, a major challenge occurs in verifying ML NFRs, including interpretability. Although existing literature defines interpretability in terms of ML, it remains an immeasurable requirement, making it impossible to definitively confirm whether a model meets its interpretability requirement. This paper shows how ML provenance can be used to verify ML interpretability requirements. This work provides an approach for how ML engineers can save various types of model and data provenance to make the model's behavior transparent and interpretable. Saving this data forms the basis of quantifiable Functional Requirements (FRs) whose verification in turn verifies the interpretability NFR. Ultimately, this paper contributes a method to verify interpretability NFRs for ML models.

</details>


### [77] [Can Large Language Models Assist the Comprehension of ROS2 Software Architectures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21699)
*Laura Duits,Bouazza El Moutaouakil,Ivano Malavolta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过1,230个提示词对9种LLM在ROS2系统架构理解方面的能力进行了受控实验，发现LLM平均准确率达98.22%，其中gemini-2.5-pro表现最佳（100%），验证了LLM在辅助ROS2开发者理解软件架构方面的巨大潜力，但需注意不同模型间的性能差异与内在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS2作为最主流的机器人软件开发框架，其架构高度复杂（包含数千个组件以去中心化方式通信），开发者理解这些架构信息具有挑战性。本文旨在评估LLM能否有效辅助ROS2系统架构事实性信息的理解。

Method: 设计了受控实验，对9个LLM模型施以1,230个关于3个不同规模ROS2系统的架构相关问题。提出了一个通用算法用于系统生成架构相关问题。通过运行和监控3个ROS2系统建立基准真值，评估LLM回答的准确性，并定性分析LLM提供的解释。

Result: 所有LLM平均准确率98.22%，gemini-2.5-pro表现最佳（100%），其后是o3（99.77%）和gemini-2.5-flash（99.72%），最差为gpt-4.1（95%）。1,230个提示中仅300个回答错误，其中249个涉及最复杂系统。LLM解释的连贯性分数在0.394（服务引用）至0.762（通信路径）之间，困惑度均值从chatgpt-4o的19.6到o4-mini的103.6不等。

Conclusion: LLM在辅助ROS2开发者理解系统非平凡架构方面具有巨大潜力，但开发者需注意LLM固有的局限性以及不同模型间的性能差异。

Abstract: Context. The most used development framework for robotics software is ROS2. ROS2 architectures are highly complex, with thousands of components communicating in a decentralized fashion. Goal. We aim to evaluate how LLMs can assist in the comprehension of factual information about the architecture of ROS2 systems. Method. We conduct a controlled experiment where we administer 1,230 prompts to 9 LLMs containing architecturally-relevant questions about 3 ROS2 systems with incremental size. We provide a generic algorithm that systematically generates architecturally-relevant questions for a ROS2 system. Then, we (i) assess the accuracy of the answers of the LLMs against a ground truth established via running and monitoring the 3 ROS2 systems and (ii) qualitatively analyse the explanations provided by the LLMs. Results. Almost all questions are answered correctly across all LLMs (mean=98.22%). gemini-2.5-pro performs best (100% accuracy across all prompts and systems), followed by o3 (99.77%), and gemini-2.5-flash (99.72%); the least performing LLM is gpt-4.1 (95%). Only 300/1,230 prompts are incorrectly answered, of which 249 are about the most complex system. The coherence scores in LLM's explanations range from 0.394 for "service references" to 0.762 for "communication path". The mean perplexity varies significantly across models, with chatgpt-4o achieving the lowest score (19.6) and o4-mini the highest (103.6). Conclusions. There is great potential in the usage of LLMs to aid ROS2 developers in comprehending non-trivial aspects of the software architecture of their systems. Nevertheless, developers should be aware of the intrinsic limitations and different performances of the LLMs and take those into account when using them.

</details>


### [78] [Automated LTL Specification Generation from Industrial Aerospace Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21715)
*Zhi Ma,Xiao Liang,Cheng Wen,Rui Chen,Bin Gu,Shengchao Qin,Cong Tian,Mengfei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AeroReq2LTL框架，利用大语言模型并引入数据字典和模板化需求语言，将航空领域自然语言需求自动转化为线性时态逻辑（LTL）属性，在真实数据集上达到85%精确率和88%召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键型航空航太软件开发中，将自然语言需求手工翻译为形式化LTL属性既耗时又易出错，且需要同时具备航空控制工程和形式化方法的罕见专业知识。现有NL-to-LTL工具在处理工业真实需求文档时，常因复杂的领域术语或隐含的时序逻辑结构而失败。

Method: 提出AeroReq2LTL框架，包含两项关键创新：(i) 数据字典，将技术行话标准化为精确的原子命题；(ii) 模板化需求语言，在翻译前将时序线索和逻辑关系显式化。利用大语言模型（LLMs）自动化生成LTL属性。

Result: 在真实航空数据集上，AeroReq2LTL在LTL生成上达到85%的精确率和88%的召回率，输出可直接被现有验证工具使用。

Conclusion: AeroReq2LTL通过结合领域知识规范化（数据字典）和结构显式化（模板化需求语言），有效提升了从自然语言到形式化LTL的自动转换质量，弥合了工业实践中的关键鸿沟。

Abstract: In the development and verification of safety-critical aero-space software, Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) has been widely used to specify complex system properties derived from requirements. However, a significant gap remains in industrial practice: translating natural language (NL) requirements into formal LTL properties is a labor-intensive and error-prone process that requires rare expertise in both aerospace control engineering and formal methods. While recent NL-to-LTL tools (e.g., NL2SPEC, NL2TL, NL2LTL) are capable of automating parts of this process, they often fail on real requirement documents in industrial settings, due to complex domain terminology or implicit temporal and logical structure. To address these challenges, we present AeroReq2LTL, a framework that automates LTL property generation for aerospace requirements using large language models (LLMs), with two key industrial innovations: (i) a data dictionary that normalizes technical jargon into precise atomic propositions; and (ii) a template-based requirement language that makes temporal cues and logical relations explicit before translation. On a real aerospace dataset, AeroReq2LTL achieves 85% precision and 88% recall in LTL generation, and its outputs can be directly consumed by existing verification tools.

</details>


### [79] [Agentic AI-assisted coding offers a unique opportunity to instill epistemic grounding during software development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21744)
*Magnus Palmblad,Jared M. Ragland,Benjamin A. Neely*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了GROUNDING$.$md，一种社区治理的、领域范围的认知基础文档，用于AI辅助软件开发的上下文中，通过编码硬约束和约定参数来确保科学正确性，使非领域专家也能生成符合最佳实践的代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI辅助编码能力飞速发展，从聊天式编程演变为完全AI辅助的智能体软件开发，当前趋势是使用除计划文档之外的其他文档（如项目和方法范围文档）。然而，缺乏一种显式的领域范围认知基础文档来编码科学正确性所需的硬约束和社区约定的默认参数，这导致非领域专家在AI辅助软件开发中难以保证输出的有效性和最佳实践。

Method: 提出了GROUNDING$.$md，一种社区治理的、领域范围的认知基础文档。该文档包含两类关键信息：Hard Constraints（硬约束，即科学正确性经验所需的不可协商的有效性不变量）和Convention Parameters（约定参数，即社区同意的默认值），这些信息覆盖所有其他上下文，强制执行有效性，无论用户提示什么。

Result: 该方法能够使非领域专家生成从一开始就内嵌最佳实践的代码、工具和软件，为软件开发者以及审查或使用最终产品的人提供信心。相比人类，智能体AI更容易遵循这些指南。

Conclusion: 该机会允许组织开发认知基础文档，使领域专家在未来的民主化定制软件解决方案生成中保持参与，在AI辅助软件开发的上下文中通过认知基础文档确保科学正确性和最佳实践。

Abstract: The capabilities of AI-assisted coding are progressing at breakneck speed. Chat-based vibe coding has evolved into fully fledged AI-assisted, agentic software development using agent scaffolds where the human developer creates a plan that agentic AIs implement. One current trend is utilizing documents beyond this plan document, such as project and method-scoped documents. Here we propose GROUNDING$.$md, a community-governed, field-scoped epistemic grounding document, using mass spectrometry-based proteomics as an example. This explicit field-scoped grounding document encodes Hard Constraints (non-negotiable validity invariants empirically required for scientific correctness) and Convention Parameters (community-agreed defaults) that override all other contexts to enforce validity, regardless of what the user prompts. In practice, this will empower a non-domain expert to generate code, tools, and software that have best practices baked in at the ground level, providing confidence to the software developer but also to those reviewing or using the final product. Undoubtedly it is easier to have agentic AIs adhere to guidelines than humans, and this opportunity allows for organizations to develop epistemic grounding documents in such a way as to keep domain experts in the loop in a future of democratized generation of bespoke software solutions.

</details>


### [80] [Generalizing Test Cases for Comprehensive Test Scenario Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21771)
*Binhang Qi,Yun Lin,Xinyi Weng,Chenyan Liu,Hailong Sun,Gordon Fraser,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TestGeneralizer框架，通过从单个初始测试用例中泛化出多个测试场景，生成覆盖更全面的测试用例，在12个Java项目上相比ChatTester在变异测试和LLM评估的场景覆盖率上分别提升31.66%和23.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在实践中需要从需求中推导多种测试场景来编写测试用例，但这一过程耗时且易错，许多重要测试被延迟添加，甚至直到缺陷触发后才补充。现有自动化测试生成技术主要关注代码覆盖率，而实际测试场景源自需求（通常未文档化且隐含在实现中），与代码分支并不对齐。

Method: TestGeneralizer包含三个阶段：(1) 增强对被测方法及初始测试背后需求与场景的理解；(2) 生成测试场景模板并将其具体化为多种测试场景实例；(3) 从场景实例生成并精炼可执行的测试用例。

Result: 在12个开源Java项目上，TestGeneralizer相比ChatTester等三个基线，在基于变异测试的场景覆盖率上提升31.66%，在LLM评估的场景覆盖率上提升23.08%。

Conclusion: 从单个初始测试出发泛化测试场景是有效的测试增强方法，能够弥补当前基于代码覆盖率的测试生成方法在实际项目中的不足，显著提升测试的全面性。

Abstract: Test cases are essential for software development and maintenance. In practice, developers derive multiple test cases from an implicit pattern based on their understanding of requirements and inference of diverse test scenarios, each validating a specific behavior of the focal method. However, producing comprehensive tests is time-consuming and error-prone: many important tests that should have accompanied the initial test are added only after a significant delay, sometimes only after bugs are triggered. Existing automated test generation techniques largely focus on code coverage. Yet in real projects, practical tests are seldom driven by code coverage alone, since test scenarios do not necessarily align with control-flow branches. Instead, test scenarios originate from requirements, which are often undocumented and implicitly embedded in a project's design and implementation. However, developer-written tests are frequently treated as executable specifications; thus, even a single initial test that reflects the developer's intent can reveal the underlying requirement and the diverse scenarios that should be validated. In this work, we propose TestGeneralizer, a framework for generalizing test cases to comprehensively cover test scenarios. TestGeneralizer orchestrates three stages: (1) enhancing the understanding of the requirement and scenario behind the focal method and initial test; (2) generating a test scenario template and crystallizing it into various test scenario instances; and (3) generating and refining executable test cases from these instances. We evaluate TestGeneralizer against three state-of-the-art baselines on 12 open-source Java projects. TestGeneralizer achieves significant improvements: +31.66% and +23.08% over ChatTester, in mutation-based and LLM-assessed scenario coverage, respectively.

</details>

### [81] [Neuromorphic Computing Based on Parametrically-Driven Oscillators and Frequency Combs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21861)
*Mahadev Sunil Kumar,Adarsh Ganesan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种双模参数驱动振荡器系统在2:1参数共振下的储层计算能力，发现参数共振区域因非线性激活与时间相干性兼具而计算性能最优，而频率梳态因相位相干性丧失导致性能下降。计算误差与分岔结构直接对应，低误差区域沿参数共振边界分布，输入调制、失谐量、阻尼比和数据速率是调控计算性能的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索参数驱动振荡器作为神经形态计算平台（特别是储层计算）的可行性，并系统揭示不同动力学区间（亚阈值、参数共振、频率梳态）对计算性能的影响机制，为物理振荡器神经形态计算提供设计准则。

Method: 采用双模系统实现2:1参数共振，将输入信号编码到驱动幅度中，采样时序和频谱响应，对Mackey-Glass、Rössler和Lorenz等混沌系统进行单步预测，系统扫描参数空间映射预测误差与分岔结构的对应关系。

Result: 参数共振区间计算性能最优；频率梳态虽具有更高谱维度但性能不稳定，且在混沌梳态下因相位相干性丧失而性能退化；预测误差与分岔结构直接对应，低误差区域沿参数共振边界分布；输入调制、失谐量、阻尼比和数据速率是决定计算性能的关键参数。

Conclusion: 参数共振是振荡器储层计算的稳健工作区间，研究结果为调控物理系统实现最优神经形态功能提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Parametrically driven oscillators provide a natural platform for neuromorphic computation, where nonlinear mode coupling and intrinsic dynamics enable both memory and high-dimensional transformation. Here, we investigate a two-mode system exhibiting 2:1 parametric resonance and demonstrate its operation as a reservoir computer across distinct dynamical regimes, including sub-threshold, parametric resonance, and frequency-comb states. By encoding input signals into the drive amplitude and sampling the resulting temporal and spectral responses, we perform one step-ahead prediction of benchmark chaotic systems, including Mackey-Glass, Rossler, and Lorenz dynamics. We find that optimal computational performance is achieved within the parametric resonance regime, where nonlinear interactions are activated while temporal coherence is preserved. In contrast, although frequency-comb states introduce increased spectral dimensionality, their performance is not consistently good across their existence band and also degrades in the chaotic comb regime due to loss of phase coherence. Mapping prediction error over parameter space reveals a direct correspondence between computational capability and the underlying bifurcation structure, with low-error regions aligned with the parametric resonance boundary. We further show that the input modulation, the detuning from the frequency matching condition, damping ratio, and input data rate systematically control the accessible dynamical regimes and thereby the computational performance. These results establish parametric resonance as a robust operating regime for oscillator-based reservoir computing and provide design principles for tuning physical systems toward optimal neuromorphic functionality.

</details>


<div id='cs.GL'></div>

# cs.GL [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [A Brief History of Fréchet Distances: From Curves and Probability Laws to FID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21745)
*Yuli Wu*

Main category: cs.GL

TL;DR: 本文追溯了Fréchet距离的发展历程：从Fr échet 1906年博士论文中抽象集合的距离概念，到1990年代多边形曲线的Fréchet距离算法计算，再到1957年基于耦合的概率分布距离（Wasserstein距离与最优传输），并连接了分布耦合层面与几何曲线层面的Fréchet距离，最后以深度生成模型评估中的Fréchet Inception Distance (FID)作为现代应用案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理Fréchet距离从抽象数学概念到现代机器学习应用（如FID）的完整历史脉络，并阐明其在概率分布（耦合视角）与几何曲线两个层面之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用历史文献追踪方法，从Fr échet 1906年博士论文出发，按照时间顺序梳理多边形曲线Fréchet距离的算法化（1990年代）、1957年概率分布距离的耦合定义，并与Wasserstein距离及最优传输理论建立关联，最后以FID作为多变量高斯分布间的Wasserstein-2距离进行现代诠释。

Result: 揭示了Fréchet距离在几何曲线和概率分布两个看似不同领域中的统一数学本质，并展示了其在深度生成模型评估（FID指标）中的实际应用价值。附录提供了Fr échet 1906年论文、1957年论文及Lévy 1950年笔记的英文翻译。

Conclusion: Fréchet距离历经一个多世纪的发展，从抽象集合的距离公理化到曲线相似性度量，再到概率分布间的Wasserstein距离，最终在深度学习的生成模型评估中找到了重要应用，体现了基础数学概念的持久生命力与跨领域价值。

Abstract: This note provides a chronological account of Fréchet distances, starting with Maurice Fréchet's 1906 doctoral thesis on distances in abstract sets and tracing the Fréchet distance between polygonal curves and its algorithmic computation in the 1990s. It then continues with his 1957 paper on a coupling-based distance between probability laws with a brief glimpse of Wasserstein distance and optimal transport. We further attempt to draw connections between the distributional, coupling-based facet of Fréchet distances on probability laws and the geometric facet on curves. The note ends with a modern use case, the Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) in the era of deep generative model evaluation, interpretable as the Wasserstein-2 distance between multivariate Gaussians in a learned feature space. An appendix includes \TeX{}ified faithful English translations of Fréchet's 1906 thesis and 1957 paper, and Lévy's 1950 note for reader convenience.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [AGNT2: Autonomous Agent Economies on Interaction-Optimized Layer 2 Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21129)
*Anbang Ruan,Xing Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出AGNT2，一个专为AI智能体和微服务协调设计的三层区块链架构，包含侧车部署模式、三层状态通道（Layer Top/Core/Root）和智能体原生执行环境，旨在解决现有L2方案无法支持高频、语义丰富的智能体服务调用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链L2方案（Optimism、Arbitrum、zkSync等）针对人类发起的金融交易优化，无法满足AI智能体之间高频、语义丰富的服务调用需求。智能体交互涉及身份、托管、依赖排序和会话状态等复杂语义，而现有链将这些视为通用calldata，迫使这些逻辑在执行层之上以错误的成本点编码。

Method: AGNT2采用三层堆栈：(1) 侧车部署模式——将Docker容器转换为链上智能体，无需修改应用代码；(2) 三层状态通道——Layer Top用于已建立的双边对（<100ms, 1K-5K TPS/对, 1000万+聚合TPS）、Layer Core用于首次联系和多方可信交互（500ms-2s, 30万-50万 TPS）、Layer Root通过计算欺诈证明锚定到EVM L1；(3) 智能体原生执行环境+交互字典树——将服务调用、身份、声誉、能力和会话上下文作为一等协议对象。

Result: 通过仿真和分析建模支持架构设计，原型测量验证了选定组件。目前实际部署受限于DA吞吐量约为1万-10万 TPS，与目标上限存在约100倍差距。尚无端到端Layer Core实现。

Conclusion: 智能体经济需要专用的执行层，而非将通用链重新用于智能体场景。AGNT2架构论证了这一方向，但端到端实现仍在进行中，DA带宽是当前主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Current blockchain Layer 2 solutions, including Optimism, Arbitrum, zkSync, and their derivatives, optimize for human-initiated financial transactions. Autonomous AI agents instead generate high-frequency, semantically rich service invocations among mutually untrusting principals. Existing chains treat those interactions as generic calldata, forcing identity, escrow, dependency ordering, and session state to be encoded above the execution layer at the wrong cost point. We present AGNT2, a three-tier stack purpose-built for agent and microservice coordination on-chain. AGNT2 combines: (1) a sidecar deployment pattern that turns any Docker container into an on-chain agent without application-code modification; (2) Layer Top P2P state channels for established bilateral pairs (<100 ms, rough design target 1K-5K TPS per pair, 10M+ aggregate TPS design envelope under endpoint-resource limits), Layer Core as a dependency-aware sequenced rollup for first-contact and multi-party interactions (500 ms-2 s, 300K-500K TPS design target), and Layer Root settlement with computational fraud proofs anchored to any EVM L1; and (3) an agent-native execution environment plus interaction trie that make service invocation, identity, reputation, capabilities, and session context first-class protocol objects. This paper focuses on the execution-layer systems problem: sequencing, state, settlement, and the data-availability (DA) bandwidth gap that bounds all three. Simulation and analytical modeling support the architecture, and prototype measurements validate selected components, but no end-to-end Layer Core implementation exists yet. Practical deployment is currently constrained to roughly 10K-100K TPS by DA throughput, leaving a ~100x gap at the target ceiling. AGNT2 argues that the agent economy requires a dedicated execution layer rather than a general-purpose chain repurposed for agents.

</details>


### [84] [Role of diversity in team performance: the case of missing expertise, an agent based simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21328)
*Tamás Kiss*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过基于主体的建模方法，研究了管理团队功能多样性（尤其是隐性分布）对团队沟通与绩效的影响。模拟实验发现，个体内功能多样性（IFD）和主导功能多样性（DFD）对团队表现的影响依赖于沟通模式和功能组成等情境因素，并且需要引入第三个指标——团队聚合专长——才能全面解释实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实验研究仅关注测量变量分布的前一两个模式，难以分析情境效应；功能多样性研究中对复杂分布的隐性效应关注不足，需要更精细的方法来揭示团队能力分布对沟通和绩效的深层影响。

Method: 采用基于主体的建模（Agent-Based Model），设计并实施一系列仿真实验，模拟管理团队中不同功能多样性构成（IFD和DFD）在不同沟通方案下的互动与表现。

Result: 1) IFD和DFD对团队绩效和沟通的影响具有情境依赖性，可能增强或削弱表现；2) 仅靠IFD和DFD两个指标不足以全面解释实证发现，需要第三个指标——团队聚合专长——来综合衡量团队整体能力。

Conclusion: 管理团队功能多样性的效应高度依赖于组织情境，未来的实证研究应考虑多维度的多样性指标（包括聚合专长），以更准确地预测团队沟通与绩效。

Abstract: Theory and empirical research on management teams' influence on firm performance have witnessed continuous development, and by now incorporate numerous details. Classic, experiment-based studies examining social systems collect vast amount of data, but often times investigate only the first one or two modes of the distribution of measured variables, and experience difficulty in analyzing the effect of context. For example, in functional diversity research, management teams are described by measures incorporating complex distributions of capabilities of individual managers and teams of managers. To investigate the effect of hidden distributions, and the effect of functional diversity composition on team communication and performance, we developed an agent-based model, and conducted a series of simulation experiments. Modeling results show that depending on the context, such as communication scheme among interacting agents, or their functional composition, intrapersonal functional diversity (IFD), and dominant function diversity (DFD) might enhance or reduce performance and communication among agents. Furthermore, simulation results also suggest that a third measure is required alongside IFD and DFD capturing the aggregate expertise of the team to comprehensively account for empirical findings.

</details>


### [85] [Agentic AI-Enabled Framework for Thermal Comfort and Building Energy Assessment in Tropical Urban Neighborhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21787)
*Po-Yen Lai,Xinyu Yang,Derrick Low,Huizhe Liu,Jian Cheng Wong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合大语言模型与轻量化物理模型的智能体AI推理框架，用于评估新加坡城市热岛效应和建筑能耗，支持通过绿墙、冷涂料等策略提升热舒适并降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对新加坡城市热岛效应和建筑能耗问题，需要一种能高效评估多种气候适应性建筑表面策略的智能化工具。

Method: 构建智能体AI框架，LLM通过提示定制理解城市设计任务、提取相关政策并激活轻量化物理模型（基于核心热力学与气流原理），形成闭环推理-行动过程，快速预测微气候变量（建筑表面温度、地面辐射热、气流条件）并估算热舒适指标（PET）及建筑能耗。

Result: 该框架能够探索绿墙、冷涂料等策略，改善热舒适、减少墙体热增益和能源需求，展示了跨学科应用的潜力。

Conclusion: LLM的自主推理能力与轻量化物理模型的快速定量评估相结合，为可持续城市设计、室内外环境集成和气候适应规划提供了有效工具。

Abstract: In response to the urban heat island effects and building energy demands in Singapore, this study proposes an agentic AI-enabled reasoning framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) with lightweight physics-based models. Through prompt customization, the LLMs interpret urban design tasks, extract relevant policies, and activate appropriate physics-based models for evaluation, forming a closed-loop reasoning-action process. These lightweight physics-based models leverage core thermal and airflow principles, streamlining conventional models to reduce computational time while predicting microclimate variables, such as building surface temperature, ground radiant heat, and airflow conditions, thereby enabling the estimation of thermal comfort indices, e.g., physiological equivalent temperature (PET), and building energy usage. This framework allows users to explore a variety of climate-resilient building surface strategies, e.g., green façades and cool paint applications, that improve thermal comfort while reducing wall heat gain and energy demand. By combining the autonomous reasoning capacity of LLMs with the rapid quantitative evaluation of lightweight physics-based models, the proposed system demonstrates potential for cross-disciplinary applications in sustainable urban design, indoor-outdoor environmental integration, and climate adaptation planning. The source code and data used in this study are available at: https://github.com/PgUpDn/urban-cooling-agent.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Frequency-Forcing: From Scaling-as-Time to Soft Frequency Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20902)
*Weitao Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Frequency-Forcing 方法，通过一个早熟的辅助低频流（基于可学习小波包变换）来引导标准像素流，在保持路径不变的前提下实现频率排序生成。该方法在 ImageNet-256 上显著优于强基线，并可无缝结合语义流进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配模型均匀地将噪声转换为数据，而引入显式生成顺序（先粗后细）已被证明对自然图像合成非常有效。现有工作 K-Flow（硬频率约束）和 Latent Forcing（软排序机制）提供了不同范式，但前者需重写流坐标，后者依赖重型预训练编码器。作者希望结合两者优势，实现一种轻量级、无需外部依赖的频率排序生成方法。

Method: Frequency-Forcing 采用 Latent Forcing 的软机制来实现 K-Flow 的频率排序：标准像素流由一个早熟的低频辅助流引导。该低频辅助流通过轻量级可学习小波包变换从数据自身派生，称为"自强制信号"，避免了外部预训练编码器的依赖，同时可学习比固定小波基更适合数据统计的基底。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 数据集上，Frequency-Forcing 一致性地改进了 FID 指标，超过了强像素空间和潜空间基线。该方法还能自然地与语义流组合，带来进一步的性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于强制（forcing）的尺度排序是一种比硬频率流更灵活、保路径的替代方案。Frequency-Forcing 验证了通过早熟辅助流引导生成这一范式的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: While standard flow-matching models transport noise to data uniformly, incorporating an explicit generation order - specifically, establishing coarse, low-frequency structure before fine detail - has proven highly effective for synthesizing natural images. Two recent works offer distinct paradigms for this. K-Flow imposes a hard frequency constraint by reinterpreting a frequency scaling variable as flow time, running the trajectory inside a transformed amplitude space. Latent Forcing provides a soft ordering mechanism by coupling the pixel flow with an auxiliary semantic latent flow via asynchronous time schedules, leaving the pixel interpolation path itself untouched. Viewed from the angle of improving pixel generation, we observe that forcing - guiding generation with an earlier-maturing auxiliary stream - offers a highly compatible route to scale-ordered generation without rewriting the core flow coordinate. Building on this, we propose Frequency-Forcing, which realizes K-Flow's frequency ordering through Latent Forcing's soft mechanism: a standard pixel flow is guided by an auxiliary low-frequency stream that matures earlier in time. Unlike Latent Forcing, whose scratchpad relies on a heavy pretrained encoder (e.g., DINO), our frequency scratchpad is derived from the data itself via a lightweight learnable wavelet packet transform. We term this a self-forcing signal, which avoids external dependencies while learning a basis better adapted to data statistics than the fixed bases used in hard frequency flows. On ImageNet-256, Frequency-Forcing consistently improves FID over strong pixel- and latent-space baselines, and naturally composes with a semantic stream to yield further gains. This illustrates that forcing-based scale ordering is a versatile, path-preserving alternative to hard frequency flows.

</details>


### [87] [Do Masked Autoencoders Improve Downhole Prediction? An Empirical Study on Real Well Drilling Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20909)
*Aleksander Berezowski,Hassan Hassanzadeh,Gouri Ginde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将掩码自编码器（MAE）预训练应用于井下钻井指标预测，在72种配置的系统搜索中，最佳MAE相比监督GRU基线降低19.8%的测试MAE，但落后监督LSTM 6.4%。关键发现：潜在空间宽度是主导架构选择，而掩码比率影响可忽略（归因于1Hz钻井数据的高时间冗余性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 井下钻井遥测存在根本性的标注不对称问题：地面传感器数据以1Hz频率连续生成，而带标注的井下测量数据成本高昂、间歇性且稀缺。当前机器学习方法普遍采用全监督从头训练，不适合这种数据 regime。

Method: 使用两个公开的Utah FORGE地热井数据集（约350万时间步的多变量钻井遥测），进行全因子设计空间搜索，涵盖72种MAE配置，并与监督LSTM和GRU基线在总泥浆体积预测任务上对比。

Result: 最佳MAE配置相比监督GRU基线降低19.8%测试MAE，但落后监督LSTM基线6.4%。潜在空间宽度是主导架构选择（Pearson r=-0.59），掩码比率影响可忽略。

Conclusion: MAE预训练可作为钻井分析的有效范式，并识别了其最有利的条件。

Abstract: Downhole drilling telemetry presents a fundamental labeling asymmetry: surface sensor data are generated continuously at 1~Hz, while labeled downhole measurements are costly, intermittent, and scarce. Current machine learning approaches for downhole metric prediction universally adopt fully supervised training from scratch, which is poorly suited to this data regime. We present the first empirical evaluation of masked autoencoder (MAE) pretraining for downhole drilling metric prediction. Using two publicly available Utah FORGE geothermal wells comprising approximately 3.5 million timesteps of multivariate drilling telemetry, we conduct a systematic full-factorial design space search across 72 MAE configurations and compare them against supervised LSTM and GRU baselines on the task of predicting Total Mud Volume. Results show that the best MAE configuration reduces test mean absolute error by 19.8\% relative to the supervised GRU baseline, while trailing the supervised LSTM baseline by 6.4\%. Analysis of design dimensions reveals that latent space width is the dominant architectural choice (Pearson $r = -0.59$ with test MAE), while masking ratio has negligible effect, an unexpected finding attributed to high temporal redundancy in 1~Hz drilling data. These results establish MAE pretraining as a viable paradigm for drilling analytics and identify the conditions under which it is most beneficial.

</details>


### [88] [FairyFuse: Multiplication-Free LLM Inference on CPUs via Fused Ternary Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20913)
*Fei Zuo,Xiaoyan Xi,Quanyi Zeng,Feiyu Wang,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairyFuse 通过融合子GEMV操作并利用AVX-512掩码加减法，在CPU上实现了无需浮点乘法的三元权重LLM推理，相比llama.cpp Q4_K_M获得1.24x加速且质量几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPU上部署LLM时，权重量化到4bit以下仍需反量化后做浮点乘法，未能充分利用三元权重{-1,0,+1}的结构优势（乘法可替换为条件加减或无操作），导致内存带宽瓶颈下的推理效率受限。

Method: 将每个全连接层的8个实值子GEMV（子广义矩阵向量乘）融合到单个AVX-512循环中，使用掩码加法和减法替代所有浮点乘法，实现零乘法执行。

Result: Roofline分析显示16倍权重压缩使内存受限的GEMV转向计算受限，核函数加速29.6倍；端到端在单Intel Xeon 8558P上达32.4 tokens/s，超过llama.cpp Q4_K_M 1.24倍，困惑度5.52 vs FP16 5.47，下游准确率66.0%。

Conclusion: 三元权重配合CPU上无乘法推理系统可在带宽受限平台实现高效LLM推理，GPU上收益有限。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed on CPU-only platforms where memory bandwidth is the primary bottleneck for autoregressive generation. Weight quantization to four bits or below reduces memory pressure, yet existing systems still dequantize weights and perform floating-point multiplications, limiting the achievable gains. Ternary weights in {-1, 0, +1} provide a more efficient alternative, replacing multiplications with conditional additions, subtractions, or no-ops. While Fairy2i shows that ternary LLMs can match FP16 quality, its runtime does not exploit this structure. We present FairyFuse, an inference system that enables multiplication-free execution on commodity CPUs by fusing the eight real-valued sub-GEMVs of each widely-linear layer into a single AVX-512 loop using masked additions and subtractions, with zero floating-point multiplications. Roofline analysis shows that 16x weight compression shifts memory-bound GEMV toward the compute regime on bandwidth-limited CPUs, yielding a 29.6x kernel speedup while offering little benefit on GPUs. End-to-end, FairyFuse achieves 32.4 tokens per second on a single Intel Xeon 8558P, outperforming llama.cpp Q4_K_M by 1.24x with near-lossless quality (WikiText-2 perplexity 5.52 vs. 5.47 FP16; downstream accuracy 66.0%).

</details>


### [89] [Towards Universal Tabular Embeddings: A Benchmark Across Data Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21696)
*Liane Vogel,Kavitha Srinivas,Niharika D'Souza,Sola Shirai,Oktie Hassanzadeh,Horst Samulowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TEmBed基准测试，系统评估表格嵌入模型在细胞、行、列、表四个表征层次的表现，发现最佳模型选择取决于具体任务和表征层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型缺乏统一的评估标准，不同方法在特定任务设置下难以直接比较，阻碍了通用表格表示模型的发展。

Method: 构建TEmBed（Tabular Embedding Test Bed）基准测试，在细胞、行、列、表四个表征层次上系统评估多种表格表示学习模型。

Result: 模型选择取决于具体任务和表征层次，没有单一模型在所有场景中表现最优。

Conclusion: 为实际应用中选择表格嵌入提供了实用指导，并为开发更通用的表格表示模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models aim to learn universal representations of tabular data that transfer across tasks and domains, enabling applications such as table retrieval, semantic search and table-based prediction. Despite the growing number of such models, it remains unclear which approach works best in practice, as existing methods are often evaluated under task-specific settings that make direct comparison difficult. To address this, we introduce TEmBed, the Tabular Embedding Test Bed, a comprehensive benchmark for systematically evaluating tabular embeddings across four representation levels: cell, row, column, and table. Evaluating a diverse set of tabular representation learning models, we show that which model to use depends on the task and representation level. Our results offer practical guidance for selecting tabular embeddings in real-world applications and lay the groundwork for developing more general-purpose tabular representation models.

</details>


### [90] [Probably Approximately Consensus: On the Learning Theory of Finding Common Ground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21811)
*Carter Blair,Ben Armstrong,Shiri Alouf-Heffetz,Nimrod Talmon,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过嵌入和降维将高维观点数据映射到一维空间，并在此空间中寻找最优共识区间的方法，同时结合经验风险最小化算法与PAC学习保证，通过选择性查询用户来高效识别广泛认同的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 deliberation 平台需要识别用户群体广泛认同的观点，但共识的获取不应仅限于用户明确表达的陈述，还应考虑不同议题的相对重要性（salience）。现有方法未能有效结合议题显著性进行共识建模。

Method: 将高维观点数据通过嵌入和降维映射到一维意见空间；定义在假设区间内最大化期望一致性的目标函数，其中期望基于议题的潜在分布（隐含了显著性）；提出高效的经验风险最小化（ERM）算法并建立PAC学习保证；通过选择性查询用户来减少所需查询次数。

Result: 初步实验验证了算法的有效性，并探索了更高效的识别最优共识区域的方法；通过选择性查询，可将所需查询次数降低到实际可行的数量。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在考虑议题显著性的前提下，高效地从高维观点数据中提取一维共识区间，具备理论保证和实际可用性。

Abstract: A primary goal of online deliberation platforms is to identify ideas that are broadly agreeable to a community of users through their expressed preferences. Yet, consensus elicitation should ideally extend beyond the specific statements provided by users and should incorporate the relative salience of particular topics. We address this issue by modelling consensus as an interval in a one-dimensional opinion space derived from potentially high-dimensional data via embedding and dimensionality reduction. We define an objective that maximizes expected agreement within a hypothesis interval where the expectation is over an underlying distribution of issues, implicitly taking into account their salience. We propose an efficient Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) algorithm and establish PAC-learning guarantees. Our initial experiments demonstrate the performance of our algorithm and examine more efficient approaches to identifying optimal consensus regions. We find that through selectively querying users on an existing sample of statements, we can reduce the number of queries needed to a practical number.

</details>


### [91] [Forget, Then Recall: Learnable Compression and Selective Unfolding via Gist Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20920)
*Yuzhen Mao,Michael Y. Li,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GSA（Gist Sparse Attention）方法，通过可学习的gist压缩令牌实现粗到细的稀疏注意力，支持端到端训练和多层级压缩，在LongBench和RAG基准上以8倍至32倍压缩率优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文场景下全注意力的二次计算成本过高，现有缓解方法（KV缓存选择或压缩）有局限，需要一种无需架构修改且端到端可学习的桥梁方案。

Method: 提出选择性展开机制：先将上下文压缩为gist令牌，选择最相关的gist，再恢复对应原始块进行细粒度注意力；通过递归gist-of-gist构建实现层级化多分辨率访问，解码复杂度为对数级别。

Result: 在LongBench和RAG基准上，以8倍至32倍压缩率一致优于其他压缩基线和推理时稀疏注意力方法。

Conclusion: GSA提供了一种有效的端到端可学习的稀疏注意力方案，结合全局压缩表示与局部细粒度证据访问，无需外部检索模块或架构修改。

Abstract: Scaling large language models to long contexts is challenging due to the quadratic computational cost of full attention. Mitigation approaches include KV-cache selection or compression techniques. We instead provide an effective and end-to-end learnable bridge between the two without requiring architecture modification. In particular, our key insight is that interleaved gist compression tokens -- which provide a learnable summary of sets of raw tokens -- can serve as routing signals for sparse attention. Building on this, we introduce selective unfolding via GSA, which first compresses the context into gist tokens, then selects the most relevant gists, and subsequently restores the corresponding raw chunks for detailed attention. This yields a simple coarse-to-fine mechanism that combines compact global representations with targeted access to fine-grained evidence. We further incorporate this process directly into training in an end-to-end fashion, avoiding the need for external retrieval modules. In addition, we extend the framework hierarchically via recursive gist-of-gist construction, enabling multi-resolution context access with logarithmic per-step decoding complexity. Empirical results on LongBench and RAG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms other compression baselines as well as inference-time sparse attention methods across compression ratios from $8\times$ to $32\times$. The code is available at: https://github.com/yuzhenmao/gist-sparse-attention/

</details>


### [92] [Validating a Deep Learning Algorithm to Identify Patients with Glaucoma using Systemic Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20921)
*John Xiang,Rohith Ravindranath,Sophia Y. Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用All of Us全国数据训练的青光眼风险评估模型，仅通过系统性电子健康记录（非眼科专科数据）即可在独立机构识别高风险青光眼患者，最佳模型AUROC达0.883，PPV为0.657，预测最高十分位组青光眼诊断率65.7%、治疗率57.0%，表明无需专科影像即可实现可扩展的青光眼预筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否仅利用系统性电子健康记录（非眼科专科检查如影像）即可在独立医疗机构的患者人群中有效识别青光眼高风险个体，从而实现更广泛、可及且低成本的青光眼预筛查。

Method: 基于All of Us全国数据预训练的青光眼风险评估模型，在Stanford医疗中心20,636名患者（2013年11月至2024年1月就诊，15%确诊青光眼）的电子健康记录上进行微调，输入特征包括人口统计学、系统性诊断、药物、实验室检查和体格检查数据，在留出测试集上评估模型性能。

Result: 最佳模型AUROC为0.883，阳性预测值（PPV）为0.657。预测概率最高十分位组青光眼诊断率65.7%，治疗率57.0%。模型性能随可训练层数增加（最多15层）以及数据量增加而提升。

Conclusion: 仅依赖系统性电子健康记录（无需专科影像）的青光眼风险评估模型能够有效识别高风险患者，具备可扩展性和可及性，有望用于大规模青光眼预筛查。

Abstract: We evaluated whether a glaucoma risk assessment (GRA) model trained on All of Us national data can identify patients at high probability of glaucoma using only systemic electronic health records (EHR) at an independent institution. In this cross-sectional study, 20,636 Stanford patients seen from November 2013 to January 2024 were included (15% with glaucoma). A pretrained GRA model was fine-tuned on the Stanford cohort and tested on a held-out set using demographics, systemic diagnoses, medications, laboratory results, and physical examination measurements as inputs. The best model achieved AUROC 0.883 and PPV 0.657. Calibration was consistent with clinical risk: the highest prediction decile showed the greatest glaucoma diagnosis rate (65.7%) and treatment rate (57.0%). Performance improved with more trainable layers up to 15 and with additional data. An EHR-only GRA model may enable scalable and accessible pre-screening without specialized imaging.

</details>


### [93] [Clinically Interpretable Sepsis Early Warning via LLM-Guided Simulation of Temporal Physiological Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20924)
*Weizhi Nie,Zhen Qu,Weijie Wang,Chunpei Li,Ke Lu,Bingyang Zhou,Hongzhi Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的时间模拟框架，通过显式建模败血症发病前的生理轨迹，实现可解释的早期预警。该框架包含时空特征提取、医学提示前缀嵌入和智能体后处理模块，在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据库上取得了优于传统方法的AUC分数（0.861-0.903），并提供可解释的轨迹和风险趋势辅助临床决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 败血症早期预警需要及时且可解释，但传统数据驱动模型虽然准确却具有黑箱性质，限制了临床医生的信任度和应用。现有方法未能有效平衡预测性能与临床可解释性之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出LLM引导的时间模拟框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 时空特征提取模块，捕捉多变量生命体征的动态依赖关系；(2) 医学提示前缀模块，将临床推理线索嵌入LLM；(3) 基于智能体的后处理组件，将预测约束在生理学合理范围内。先模拟关键生理指标的演变，再进行败血症发病分类。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU数据库上，该方法在24至4小时提前预测任务中取得AUC 0.861-0.903，优于传统深度学习方法和基于规则的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可解释的生理轨迹和风险趋势，能够辅助临床医生在重症监护环境中进行早期干预和个性化决策，实现了预测准确性与临床可解释性的有效结合。

Abstract: Timely and interpretable early warning of sepsis remains a major clinical challenge due to the complex temporal dynamics of physiological deterioration. Traditional data-driven models often provide accurate yet opaque predictions, limiting physicians' confidence and clinical applicability. To address this limitation, we propose a Large Language Model (LLM)-guided temporal simulation framework that explicitly models physiological trajectories prior to disease onset for clinically interpretable prediction. The framework consists of a spatiotemporal feature extraction module that captures dynamic dependencies among multivariate vital signs, a Medical Prompt-as-Prefix module that embeds clinical reasoning cues into LLMs, and an agent-based post-processing component that constrains predictions within physiologically plausible ranges. By first simulating the evolution of key physiological indicators and then classifying sepsis onset, our model offers transparent prediction mechanisms that align with clinical judgment. Evaluated on the MIMIC-IV and eICU databases, the proposed method achieves superior AUC scores (0.861-0.903) across 24-4-hour pre-onset prediction tasks, outperforming conventional deep learning and rule-based approaches. More importantly, it provides interpretable trajectories and risk trends that can assist clinicians in early intervention and personalized decision-making in intensive care environments.

</details>


### [94] [Domain-Aware Hierarchical Contrastive Learning for Semi-Supervised Generalization Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20928)
*Junyu Ren,Wensheng Gan,Philip S Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对半监督域泛化故障诊断中伪标签偏差和样本利用不均衡的问题，提出域感知分层对比学习（DAHCL）框架，通过域感知学习模块校正跨域伪标签偏差，并结合分层对比学习模块实现不确定性样本的有效利用，在多个基准数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督域泛化故障诊断方法存在两个耦合缺陷：一是伪标签生成仅依赖有标签源域知识，忽略域间几何差异导致系统性跨域偏差；二是对无标签样本采用硬性接受或丢弃策略，导致跨域样本利用不均衡，且硬标签分配对不确定样本引入额外噪声。

Method: 提出DAHCL统一框架：(1) 域感知学习(DAL)模块显式捕获源域几何特征并校准异构源域的伪标签预测，缓解伪标签生成中的跨域偏差；(2) 分层对比学习(HCL)模块结合动态置信度分层与模糊对比监督，使不确定样本无需依赖不可靠硬标签即可参与表示学习。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了DAHCL的有效性，论文还引入了工程噪声以更好地反映实际工业场景。

Conclusion: DAHCL通过联合改善监督质量和无标签样本利用率，有效解决了半监督域泛化故障诊断中的伪标签偏差和样本利用不均衡问题，为实际工业场景下的故障诊断提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis under unseen operating conditions remains highly challenging when labeled data are scarce. Semi-supervised domain generalization fault diagnosis (SSDGFD) provides a practical solution by jointly exploiting labeled and unlabeled source domains. However, existing methods still suffer from two coupled limitations. First, pseudo-labels for unlabeled domains are typically generated primarily from knowledge learned on the labeled source domain, which neglects domain-specific geometric discrepancies and thus induces systematic cross-domain pseudo-label bias. Second, unlabeled samples are commonly handled with a hard accept-or-discard strategy, where rigid thresholding causes imbalanced sample utilization across domains, while hard-label assignment for uncertain samples can easily introduce additional noise. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework termed domain-aware hierarchical contrastive learning (DAHCL) for SSDGFD. Specifically, DAHCL introduces a domain-aware learning (DAL) module to explicitly capture source-domain geometric characteristics and calibrate pseudo-label predictions across heterogeneous source domains, thereby mitigating cross-domain bias in pseudo-label generation. In addition, DAHCL develops a hierarchical contrastive learning (HCL) module that combines dynamic confidence stratification with fuzzy contrastive supervision, enabling uncertain samples to contribute to representation learning without relying on unreliable hard labels. In this way, DAHCL jointly improves the quality of supervision and the utilization of unlabeled samples. Furthermore, to better reflect practical industrial scenarios, we incorporate engineering noise into the SSDGFD evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that...

</details>


### [95] [Data-Driven Open-Loop Simulation for Digital-Twin Operator Decision Support in Wastewater Treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20935)
*Gary Simethy,Daniel Ortiz Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CCSS-RS，一种受控连续时间状态空间模型，用于全规模污水处理厂的数字孪生决策支持。在Avedøre基准上（90.6万时间步，43%缺失率），RMSE 0.696，比Neural CDE基线和简化变体分别降低40-46%和31-35%。四个案例研究验证了其在离线场景筛选中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 污水处理厂需要数字孪生决策支持工具，能够模拟预设控制方案下的工厂响应、容忍不规则和缺失的传感数据，并在12-36小时规划范围内保持信息有效性。用全规模工厂数据满足这些需求仍是一个开放的工程-AI挑战。

Method: CCSS-RS：受控连续时间状态空间模型，将历史状态推断与未来控制和外生变量展开分离。结合类型化上下文编码、增益加权强制驱动、半群一致展开，以及Student-t加hurdle输出分布以适应重尾和零膨胀的污水处理传感器数据。

Result: 在Avedøre全规模基准上（906,815时间步，43%缺失率，1-20分钟不规则采样），H=1000时RMSE 0.696，CRPS 0.349。相比Neural CDE基线RMSE降低40-46%，相比内部简化变体降低31-35%。四个案例研究：氧设定点扰动使铵预测变化-2.3至+1.4；平滑设定点方案在多准则筛选中排名第一；传感器仅上下文缺失使RMSE最多增加10%；铵、硝酸盐和氧的精度在整个展开中优于持久性模型。

Conclusion: CCSS-RS被确立为工业污水处理中一种实用的离线场景筛选学习模拟器，与机理模型互补。

Abstract: Wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) need digital-twin-style decision support tools that can simulate plant response under prescribed control plans, tolerate irregular and missing sensing, and remain informative over 12-36 h planning horizons. Meeting these requirements with full-scale plant data remains an open engineering-AI challenge. We present CCSS-RS, a controlled continuous-time state-space model that separates historical state inference from future control and exogenous rollout. The model combines typed context encoding, gain-weighted forcing of prescribed and forecast drivers, semigroup-consistent rollouts, and Student-t plus hurdle outputs for heavy-tailed and zero-inflated WWTP sensor data. On the public Avedøre full-scale benchmark, with 906,815 timesteps, 43% missingness, and 1-20 min irregular sampling, CCSS-RS achieves RMSE 0.696 and CRPS 0.349 at H=1000 across 10,000 test windows. This reduces RMSE by 40-46% relative to Neural CDE baselines and by 31-35% relative to simplified internal variants. Four case studies using a frozen checkpoint on test data demonstrate operational value: oxygen-setpoint perturbations shift predicted ammonium by -2.3 to +1.4 over horizons 300-1000; a smoothed setpoint plan ranks first in multi-criterion screening; context-only sensor outages raise monitored-variable RMSE by at most 10%; and ammonium, nitrate, and oxygen remain more accurate than persistence throughout the rollout. These results establish CCSS-RS as a practical learned simulator for offline scenario screening in industrial wastewater treatment, complementary to mechanistic models.

</details>


### [96] [Sink-Token-Aware Pruning for Fine-Grained Video Understanding in Efficient Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20937)
*Kibum Kim,Jiwan Kim,Kyle Min,Yueqi Wang,Jinyoung Moon,Julian McAuley,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有训练无关的视觉Token剪枝方法在细粒度视频理解任务（如幻觉评估）上性能大幅下降，原因是存在"汇合Token"（sink tokens）——语义信息量少却吸引过多注意力的Token。本文提出SToP方法，通过引入汇合得分量化并抑制这些Token，在剪枝高达90%视觉Token时仍能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的视觉Token剪枝方法主要在粗粒度的多选题问答基准上验证，但在需要精确视觉定位的细粒度理解任务（如幻觉评估）上性能严重下降。本文旨在揭示这一性能差距的原因并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出Sink-Token-aware Pruning (SToP)，一种即插即用的方法。首先引入汇合得分（sink score）量化每个Token成为汇合Token的倾向性，然后将该得分应用于现有的空间和时间剪枝方法中，抑制汇合Token在剪枝后残留。

Result: 将SToP应用于VisionZip、FastVid和Holitom三种SOTA剪枝方法，在幻觉评估、开放生成、组合推理和MCQA等多种基准上验证，结果显示即使在剪枝90%视觉Token时仍能显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 汇合Token是阻碍细粒度视频理解的键障碍，通过识别并抑制这些Token，SToP能有效改善训练无关剪枝方法在细粒度任务上的性能退化。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) incur high inference latency due to a large number of visual tokens provided to LLMs. To address this, training-free visual token pruning has emerged as a solution to reduce computational costs; however, existing methods are primarily validated on Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) benchmarks, where coarse-grained cues often suffice. In this work, we reveal that these methods suffer a sharp performance collapse on fine-grained understanding tasks requiring precise visual grounding, such as hallucination evaluation. To explore this gap, we conduct a systematic analysis and identify sink tokens--semantically uninformative tokens that attract excessive attention--as a key obstacle to fine-grained video understanding. When these sink tokens survive pruning, they distort the model's visual evidence and hinder fine-grained understanding. Motivated by these insights, we propose Sink-Token-aware Pruning (SToP), a simple yet effective plug-and-play method that introduces a sink score to quantify each token's tendency to behave as a sink and applies this score to existing spatial and temporal pruning methods to suppress them, thereby enhancing video understanding. To validate the effectiveness of SToP, we apply it to state-of-the-art pruning methods (VisionZip, FastVid, and Holitom) and evaluate it across diverse benchmarks covering hallucination, open-ended generation, compositional reasoning, and MCQA. Our results demonstrate that SToP significantly boosts performance, even when pruning up to 90% of visual tokens.

</details>


### [97] [SCM: Sleep-Consolidated Memory with Algorithmic Forgetting for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20943)
*Saish Sachin Shinde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种受神经科学启发的睡眠巩固记忆架构（SCM），包含工作记忆、重要性标记、NREM/REM睡眠阶段巩固、基于价值的遗忘和自省模型，在8项标准化测试中实现十轮对话完美召回率，记忆噪声降低90.9%，搜索延迟低于1毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型缺乏持久、结构化且具有生物学合理性的记忆机制，现有方法依赖截断上下文窗口、无边界增长的向量数据库或缺乏巩固与遗忘机制的分层存储系统。

Method: 设计了五个核心组件：有限容量工作记忆、多维重要性标记、离线睡眠阶段巩固（区分NREM和REM阶段）、基于意图的价值驱动遗忘、以及支持内省的计算自我模型。

Result: 在八项标准化基准测试中，十轮对话召回准确率完美；通过自适应遗忘将记忆噪声降低90.9%；即使存储数百个概念，记忆搜索延迟仍低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了能够巩固、优先化和遗忘的记忆系统架构基础，为推进LLM记忆研究提供了可测试的平台。

Abstract: We present SCM (Sleep-Consolidated Memory), a research preview of a memory architecture for large language models that draws on neuroscientific principles to address a fundamental limitation in current systems: the absence of persistent, structured, and biologically plausible memory. Existing approaches rely on truncating context windows, growing vector databases without bound, or tiered storage systems that lack consolidation and forgetting mechanisms. SCM implements five core components inspired by human memory: a limited-capacity working memory, multi-dimensional importance tagging, offline sleep-stage consolidation with distinct NREM and REM phases, intentional value-based forgetting, and a computational self-model enabling introspection. Across a standardized benchmark suite of eight tests, the prototype achieves perfect recall accuracy over ten-turn conversations while reducing memory noise by 90.9% through adaptive forgetting. Memory search latency remains below one millisecond even with hundreds of stored concepts. This work establishes the architectural foundations for memory systems that consolidate, prioritize, and forget, offering a testable platform for advancing LLM memory research.

</details>


### [98] [LAF-Based Evaluation and UTTL-Based Learning Strategies with MIATTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20944)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对真实世界中因模糊性或主观性导致真实目标无法精确定义的问题，提出了EL-MIATTs框架。在该框架下，开发了基于LAF（逻辑评估公式）的评估算法和基于UTTL（未定义真实目标学习）的学习策略，用于处理多个不准确真实目标下的逻辑一致且实际可行的建模。文章分析了MIATTs的覆盖度与多样性对结构特性的影响，提出了基于LAF的评估算法（可基于原始MIATTs或合成的三元目标运行），以及基于UTTL的学习策略（使用Dice和交叉熵损失函数）。LAF与UTTL的整合弥合了逻辑语义与统计优化之间的鸿沟，为“真实标注”本身不确定的ML系统提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多现实机器学习应用中，真实目标因信息模糊或主观性而无法精确定义，传统依赖客观“真实标注”的监督学习方法难以适用。EL-MIATTs框架旨在解决这一挑战。

Method: (1) 分析任务特定MIATTs的覆盖度和多样性，揭示其结构特性对评估和学习的影响；(2) 提出LAF-based评估算法，可作用于原始MIATTs或合成的三元目标，兼顾可解释性、合理性和完备性；(3) 提出UTTL-based学习策略，使用Dice和交叉熵损失函数，对比按目标和聚合优化方案；(4) 整合LAF和UTTL以弥合逻辑语义与统计优化之间的鸿沟。

Result: 提出了两种互补机制：LAF-based评估算法和UTTL-based学习策略，共同实现了在不确定监督下的逻辑一致且实际可行的建模。应用成果可在https://www.qeios.com/read/EZWLSN查看。

Conclusion: 本文为EL-MIATTs框架的实现提供了从理论到实践的连贯路径，为“真实标注”本身不确定的机器学习系统奠定了有原理支撑的基础。

Abstract: In many real-world machine learning (ML) applications, the true target cannot be precisely defined due to ambiguity or subjectivity information. To address this challenge, under the assumption that the true target for a given ML task is not assumed to exist objectively in the real world, the EL-MIATTs (Evaluation and Learning with Multiple Inaccurate True Targets) framework has been proposed. Bridging theory and practice in implementing EL-MIATTs, in this paper, we develop two complementary mechanisms: LAF (Logical Assessment Formula)-based evaluation algorithms and UTTL (Undefinable True Target Learning)-based learning strategies with MIATTs, which together enable logically coherent and practically feasible modeling under uncertain supervision. We first analyze task-specific MIATTs, examining how their coverage and diversity determine their structural property and influence downstream evaluation and learning. Based on this understanding, we formulate LAF-grounded evaluation algorithms that operate either on original MIATTs or on ternary targets synthesized from them, balancing interpretability, soundness, and completeness. For model training, we introduce UTTL-grounded learning strategies using Dice and cross-entropy loss functions, comparing per-target and aggregated optimization schemes. We also discuss how the integration of LAF and UTTL bridges the gap between logical semantics and statistical optimization. Together, these components provide a coherent pathway for implementing EL-MIATTs, offering a principled foundation for developing ML systems in scenarios where the notion of "ground truth" is inherently uncertain. An application of this work's results is presented as part of the study available at https://www.qeios.com/read/EZWLSN.

</details>


### [99] [Differentially Private Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20985)
*Qichuan Yin,Manzil Zaheer,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种后处理技术（随机选择和线性组合），利用已训练好的不同隐私/效用权衡模型，无需额外训练即可生成满足任意目标差分隐私要求的模型，并通过Rényi DP和隐私损失分布进行隐私核算，在私有均值估计中理论上证明了线性组合优于随机选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际机器学习应用中，推理或部署阶段的隐私要求可能因政策、法规或用户体验的变化而动态调整，现有方法需要针对每个新隐私需求重新训练模型，效率低下。

Method: 提出两种后处理技术：1) 随机选择：从已有模型中按某种概率选取一个作为输出；2) 线性组合：对多个已有模型的参数进行加权线性组合。基于Rényi DP和隐私损失分布框架进行隐私核算。

Result: 在私有均值估计问题中完整刻画了隐私/效用结果，理论上证明线性组合优于随机选择。在合成和真实数据集上的多个模型实验中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用一组已有的不同隐私/效用权衡模型，通过简单的后处理技术可以灵活满足任意目标隐私需求，无需重新训练，其中线性组合方法在理论上和实证上均表现更优。

Abstract: In machine learning applications, privacy requirements during inference or deployment time could change constantly due to varying policies, regulations, or user experience. In this work, we aim to generate a magnitude of models to satisfy any target differential privacy (DP) requirement without additional training steps, given a set of existing models trained on the same dataset with different privacy/utility tradeoffs. We propose two post processing techniques, namely random selection and linear combination, to output a final private model for any target privacy parameter. We provide privacy accounting of these approaches from the lens of R'enyi DP and privacy loss distributions for general problems. In a case study on private mean estimation, we fully characterize the privacy/utility results and theoretically establish the superiority of linear combination over random selection. Empirically, we validate our approach and analyses on several models and both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [100] [mcdok at SemEval-2026 Task 13: Finetuning LLMs for Detection of Machine-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21365)
*Adam Skurla,Dominik Macko,Jakub Simko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对SemEval-2026 Task 13中多语言机器生成代码片段的检测任务，将原本用于机器生成文本检测的mdok方法适配到代码领域，通过探索更适合代码理解的基础模型，在二元检测、来源归属、LLM家族检测以及人机混合/对抗性代码检测三个子任务上均取得了有竞争力的结果，但与顶级系统仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域、多编程语言的机器生成代码片段检测是一项具有挑战性的任务，现有方法主要针对自然语言文本，缺乏对代码特性的适配。

Method: 将原有的mdok方法（专注于机器生成文本检测）进行调整，通过探索更适合代码理解的各种基础模型，适配到代码片段检测任务中。

Result: 所提交系统在所有三个子任务（二元检测、来源归属/LLM家族检测、人机混合/对抗性代码检测）上均表现出竞争力，但与顶级系统之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 所提方法具有竞争力，但仍有进一步改进的空间，尤其是在缩小与顶级系统的差距方面。

Abstract: Multi-domain detection of the machine-generated code snippets in various programming languages is a challenging task. SemEval-2026 Task~13 copes with this challenge in various angles, as a binary detection problem as well as attribution of the source. Specifically, its subtasks also cover generator LLM family detection, as well as a hybrid code co-generated by humans and machines, or adversarially modified codes hiding its origin. Our submitted systems adjusted the existing mdok approach (focused on machine-generated text detection) to these specific kinds of problems by exploring various base models, more suitable for code understanding. The results indicate that the submitted systems are competitive in all three subtasks. However, the margins from the top-performing systems are significant, and thus further improvements are possible.

</details>


### [101] [SGD at the Edge of Stability: The Stochastic Sharpness Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21016)
*Fangshuo Liao,Afroditi Kolomvaki,Anastasios Kyrillidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机自稳定理论，解释了小批量SGD中锐度稳定在2/η以下的现象，推导出锐度间隙的闭式解ΔS = ηβσ_u²/(4α)，证明了更小的批量导致更平坦的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GD全批量训练中锐度稳定在2/η的EoS现象已被解释，但SGD中锐度稳定在2/η以下且随批量减小间隙增大的现象缺乏理论解释。

Method: 将自稳定框架扩展到SGD，引入随机自稳定概念；定义相对于移动投影梯度下降轨迹的随机预测动力学；证明随机耦合定理，界定了SGD与预测轨迹的偏差；推导出均衡锐度间隙的闭式解。

Result: 推导出锐度间隙公式ΔS = ηβσ_u²/(4α)，其中α为渐进锐化率，β为自稳定强度，σ_u²为梯度噪声在顶特征向量上的投影方差。该公式预测更小批量产生更平坦的解，当批量为全数据集时退化为GD结果。

Conclusion: 梯度噪声沿顶特征向量向振荡动力学注入方差，增强了三次方锐度减小力，使均衡移到2/η以下。理论预测与经验观察一致。

Abstract: When training neural networks with full-batch gradient descent (GD) and step size $η$, the largest eigenvalue of the Hessian -- the sharpness $S(\boldsymbolθ)$ -- rises to $2/η$ and hovers there, a phenomenon termed the Edge of Stability (EoS). \citet{damian2023selfstab} showed that this behavior is explained by a self-stabilization mechanism driven by third-order structure of the loss, and that GD implicitly follows projected gradient descent (PGD) on the constraint $ S(\boldsymbolθ)\leq 2/η$. For mini-batch stochastic gradient descent (SGD), the sharpness stabilizes below $2/η$, with the gap widening as the batch size decreases; yet no theoretical explanation exists for this suppression.
  We introduce stochastic self-stabilization, extending the self-stabilization framework to SGD. Our key insight is that gradient noise injects variance into the oscillatory dynamics along the top Hessian eigenvector, strengthening the cubic sharpness-reducing force and shifting the equilibrium below $2/η$. Following the approach of \citet{damian2023selfstab}, we define stochastic predicted dynamics relative to a moving projected gradient descent trajectory and prove a stochastic coupling theorem that bounds the deviation of SGD from these predictions. We derive a closed-form equilibrium sharpness gap: $ΔS = ηβσ_{\boldsymbol{u}}^{2}/(4α)$, where $α$ is the progressive sharpening rate, $β$ is the self-stabilization strength, and $σ_{ \boldsymbol{u}}^{2}$ is the gradient noise variance projected onto the top eigenvector. This formula predicts that smaller batch sizes yield flatter solutions and recovers GD when the batch equals the full dataset.

</details>


### [102] [PrismaDV: Automated Task-Aware Data Unit Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21765)
*Hao Chen,Arnab Phani,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismaDV是一个复合AI系统，通过分析下游任务代码和数据集特征，生成任务感知的可执行数据单元测试，并利用SIFTA框架自动优化提示词，在60个任务上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化数据单元测试框架是任务无关的——它们验证数据集时没有考虑消费数据的代码的语义和需求，导致无法有效检测对下游应用有实际影响的数据错误。

Method: 提出PrismaDV复合AI系统，分析下游任务代码和数据集特征以识别数据访问模式、推断隐式数据假设，生成任务感知的可执行数据单元测试；并提出SIFTA（选择性信息反馈用于任务适应）提示优化框架，利用数据单元测试和下游任务执行的稀疏结果自动学习提示词。

Result: 在两个新基准测试（涵盖5个数据集的60个任务）上，PrismaDV始终优于任务无关和任务感知基线；SIFTA自动学习的提示词优于手工编写或通用优化器生成的提示词。

Conclusion: 任务感知的数据单元测试生成方法能更准确地反映数据错误的端到端影响，且通过稀疏反馈自动优化提示词是有效的，为数据质量保障提供了新范式。

Abstract: Data is a central resource for modern enterprises, and data validation is essential for ensuring the reliability of downstream applications. However, existing automated data unit testing frameworks are largely task-agnostic: they validate datasets without considering the semantics and requirements of the code that consumes the data.
  We present PrismaDV, a compound AI system that analyzes downstream task code together with dataset profiles to identify data access patterns, infer implicit data assumptions, and generate task-aware executable data unit tests. To further adapt the data unit tests over time to specific datasets and downstream tasks, we propose "Selective Informative Feedback for Task Adaptation" (SIFTA), a prompt-optimization framework that leverages the scarce outcomes from the execution of data unit tests and downstream tasks. We evaluate PrismaDV on two new benchmarks spanning 60 tasks across five datasets, where it consistently outperforms both task-agnostic and task-aware baselines in generating unit tests that reflect the end-to-end impact of data errors. Furthermore, we show that with SIFTA, we can automatically learn prompts for PrismaDV's modules that outperform prompts written by hand or generated from a generic prompt optimizer. We publicly release our benchmarks and prototype implementation.

</details>


### [103] [MCAP: Deployment-Time Layer Profiling for Memory-Constrained LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21026)
*Anurita Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCAP方法，通过负载时逐层重要性估计实现动态精度和内存放置决策，NVE系统在异构硬件上比llama.cpp Q4_0提升1.5-1.8倍解码吞吐量，且无需修改权重即可在原本不可行的内存预算下运行模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署到异构硬件时，性能瓶颈往往受限于内存而非计算能力，需要一种轻量级方法在目标设备上实现动态精度和内存放置决策。
